- Platform Infra PR [#1019](https://github.com/phanthienquoc/platform-infra/pull/1019) fixed issue [#1015](https://github.com/phanthienquoc/platform-infra/issues/1015) and was squash-merged as `15c46d1093321a5a5b7570686d9222edfdf564f3` from exact head `58a189efd7e9cb9f61e5654b659509081dc078fd`. - The platform-owned `platform-kubectl` helper now inspects selected container and init-container waiting messages. It skips rollout restart for permanent image-reference/architecture failures (`no match for platform in manifest`, `manifest unknown`, `manifest not found`), while retaining retries for non-permanent ImagePullBackOff/ErrImagePull failures after shared GHCR credential reconciliation. A Deployment with both permanent and transient errors is fail-closed and not restarted.
- Regression coverage now exercises permanent architecture/manifest failures, transient auth/network/rate-limit failures, mixed permanent+transient failures, and unrelated CrashLoopBackOff. The existing `validate-github-actions` workflow runs the test and workflow contract assertions; shellcheck and all required exact-head checks passed.
- Runner policy is now `rollout-status-v13-permanent-image-pull-restart`. `reconcile-vps.yml` requires exact v13 before any production apply; standalone Auth provisioning accepts v13 while retaining the documented older compatibility values. The existing `bootstrap-runner.yml` remains the only installer and is path-triggered by `scripts/bootstrap-runner.sh`. **The post-merge bootstrap run and live VPS `policy-version` have not yet been verified**, so do not claim v13 is installed. ### New confirmed issue: permanent StockDividend image errors cause pointless rollout restarts

- In reconciliation #41351121366, the existing `Restart target workloads blocked on image pulls` step invoked the platform-owned helper with `TARGET_APP=all`.
- Runner logs show `stock-prod/stock-admin` restarted at 21:16:23Z and `stock-prod/stock-backend` restarted at 21:16:24Z solely because their waiting reasons were `ImagePullBackOff`/`ErrImagePull`.
- The same run's health gate reported `admin:12d63b4` and `backend:12d63b4` fail with `no match for platform in manifest`. This is a permanent image architecture/reference problem, not a transient credential failure.
- Fresh snapshot #41351555469 at 21:21:06Z showed two blocked Admin pods, a newly created blocked Backend pod, and the previous Backend replica still Running. The helper currently inspects waiting reasons but not `.state.waiting.message`, so repeated all-app reconciliations can create additional failing pods without changing the image.
- Tracked as [Platform Infra issue #1015](https://github.com/phanthienquoc/platform-infra/issues/1015). Required fix: inspect waiting messages; skip restart for permanent architecture/manifest errors (`no match for platform in manifest`, `manifest unknown`, `manifest not found`); retain retries for transient auth/network/rate-limit failures; add mocked-kubectl regression tests; bump the runner policy and update both reconciliation and standalone Auth policy gates. Keep `bootstrap-runner.yml` as the single helper installation authority. Do not change production manifests, image refs, or desired replicas as part of this fix.
- No runner helper change was merged or installed in this review. Production restart behavior remains as-is until issue #1015 is implemented and validated.

Targeted reconcile [#41191877875](https://github.com/phanthienquoc/platform-infra/actions/runs/41191877875) then resolved `target_app=auth-shutdown`, skipped shared GHCR validation/credential rotation/restarts and full-environment apply, applied only `platform/keycloak`, scaled `microfe-auth` and `keycloak` to zero, and passed the postflight gate requiring desired/ready/updated replicas zero and no matching pods. ## Target operating model

- **Application repositories** own source tests, build verification, immutable ARM64 GHCR images, and release dispatch only.
- **platform-infra** owns production Kustomize manifests, shared GHCR pull credentials, image validation/promotion, reconciliation, rollout health checks, and deployment notifications.
- Never let an application repository both deploy directly to the VPS and request GitOps promotion for the same production runtime.
- Production health is only PASS when current Kubernetes rollout and application health evidence is available.

## Previous verification (2026-10-31 15:01 ICT)

- **Temporary TCE shutdown:** PR [#883](https://github.com/phanthienquoc/platform-infra/pull/883) merged as `b04c433fd8a54a30104fe8e9ec7ed1d4da3fc902`. Shared reconciliation and post-apply drift verification passed in [#41180227511](https://github.com/phanthienquoc/platform-infra/actions/runs/41180227511). The TCE lane's rollout commands succeeded, and its health step then reported no pods found because both Deployments were intentionally scaled to zero. This run supports that the shutdown was applied at 14:22 ICT, but it did not pass the aggregate health gate and no later live snapshot proves the current state.
- **Health-gate defect identified:** `scripts/verify-workload-health.sh` assumes every targeted namespace must contain an active pod. The fail-closed maintenance contract must require desired=0, ready=0, updated=0, and no remaining active pods before classifying a lane as intentionally paused. A dedicated branch adds this behavior and regression cases for converged scale-down, non-converged zero replicas, and stale active pods.
- **Other production lanes:** the same reconcile failed MicroFE, StockDividend, Auth, and Media health gates. Their failures are independent; no release gate or runtime credential was changed. Observability workflow [#41180951147](https://github.com/phanthienquoc/platform-infra/actions/runs/41180951147) passed but is not production health evidence.

## Latest Auth database evidence (2026-10-31 16:18 ICT)

- Supabase project `gtqovpusfyyvxpeezxlo` reports control-plane status `ACTIVE_HEALTHY`, but direct read-only SQL ping and `pg_roles` lookup still fail with `Connection terminated due to connection timeout`; database query health and the existence/login state of `keycloak_runtime` remain unverified.
- Supavisor log query works. In the 10 minutes ending 2026-10-31 09:18:13 UTC, it returned 382 `auth_error` circuit-breaker events for user `keycloak_runtime` in session mode from one peer IP; the log says too many authentication failures and new connections are temporarily blocked. This is stronger evidence of repeated authentication failure than a general database outage, but it does not alone prove whether the role password or the Kubernetes Secret is stale.
- The production Auth secret check verifies only required key presence and the expected JDBC target/user; it suppresses values and does not validate the password against PostgreSQL. Do not print or copy the password into logs/chat. Next safe step is to compare/reset the role and secret through approved secure controls, then stop the retry loop and re-test SQL/auth connectivity. No role, password, Secret, or workload was changed in this run.
- Production remains **NOT HEALTHY/UNVERIFIED**: `microfe-auth` is desired=1/ready=0, and public Auth liveness/readiness returns HTTP 503. TCE remains intentionally stopped; its market-scan schedule is untouched.

## Workflow inventory and disposition

Disposition meanings:
- **KEEP**: distinct CI, release, maintenance, security, or operational function.
- **CHANGE**: deployment authority or release contract must be aligned.
- **REVIEW**: potentially redundant or one-off; do not delete until current consumers and recent usage are verified.
- **REMOVE**: confirmed no-op/obsolete duplicate with no remaining consumer.

## Deployment invariants

1. Every production image tag referenced by a dispatch payload must be published by the same run.
2. A GitOps event must target an app/component accepted by `gitops-image-promotion.yml`.
3. Application repos must not mutate production directly over SSH when platform-infra owns the K3s workload.
4. Build/test workflows must not publish or deploy production images on ordinary PRs.
5. Keep credentials in GitHub Secrets/Environments or Kubernetes Secrets; never put values in logs, payloads, or manifests.
6. A workflow being present or a dispatch succeeding is not proof of production health; verify image pull, rollout, readiness, ingress and application health.

- Current source inspection found `reconcile-vps.yml` granting `packages: write` to its workflow token despite all private image reads using the protected `GHCR_READ_TOKEN` secret.

This is a workflow/security-only change; it does not modify production manifests, registry tokens, or runtime credentials.

### TCE digest-pinned producer/consumer contract — merged

- TCE producer PR [#1013](https://github.com/phanthienquoc/TCE-dashboard/pull/1013) merged as `afad281fa57b2846446a152b6f5cf3c2600ee51d`.
- Platform consumer PR [#1065](https://github.com/phanthienquoc/platform-infra/pull/1065) merged as `f30e99d6e6bea69878d5f754886edc8a0a82b54a`.
- Platform runtime compatibility PR [#1066](https://github.com/phanthienquoc/platform-infra/pull/1066) merged as `0148a24be26f73661a63c5e620589c8351fa044a`.

TCE producer now uses a full 40-character source SHA in the release tag, resolves web and service GHCR digests after push, verifies each published manifest includes `linux/arm64`, and sends both digests with the source-run provenance fields in `tce-release-requested`.

Platform's `promote-tce-digests` consumer verifies the source Actions run, full-SHA release label, tag-to-digest mapping, exact GHCR digest references and ARM64 support before changing GitOps. It writes both web/service `digest:` fields atomically in one production-overlay PR, validates the exact PR head and required checks, squash-merges only at that head, then waits for both digests on master before dispatching the shared reconciliation authority.

The new `scripts/promote-tce-image-digests.py` updater has offline positive/negative self-tests. The changed-image contract now rejects tag-based TCE production image changes; the manifest renderer still accepts the existing legacy tag pair until the next authorized digest release.

### TCE reconciliation compatibility — merged

PR #1066 adds `scripts/resolve-tce-gitops-image-refs.py`, which validates either:
- a matching legacy web/service release-tag pair; or
- a pair of valid SHA-256 digests.

It rejects mixed tag/digest modes, mismatched legacy tags, malformed digests, duplicate fields and missing image entries. Both `scripts/verify-tce-gitops-release.sh` and `scripts/verify-tce-gitops-images.sh` use the resolver; the live-image verifier compares each Deployment image reference using the correct `:` tag or `@` digest form. The resolver's offline regression suite is part of `validate-github-actions`.

### Watchdog regression found and fix proposed in #1076
- Latest workflow_run watchdog #41499180006 failed at 18:17:38Z. Its inspection freshness check passed against the newly resumed native inspection #41498271871 (age 5m), but reconciliation freshness failed because latest native reconcile #41491502003 was 49m old (>45m). The deployment-lane queue step also re-failed the same historical completed/assigned delays from #41491502003: MicroFE 31m, TCE 32m and Media 32m.
- The parent reconciliation is already stale and its primary freshness gate reports that failure. Re-evaluating completed runner-assignment delays on every later workflow_run caused a permanent repeated watchdog failure for the same old run.
- PR #1076 adds parent reconciliation age to the read-only queue verifier. For a stale parent (>45m), completed/already-assigned historical lane delays are not re-enforced; a lane still queued/unassigned remains actionable and still fails beyond the 15m queue budget. Fresh parent runs continue enforcing completed runner-assignment delays. The existing stale-reconciliation freshness check remains authoritative.
- Added two regression cases: stale parent + completed historical delay returns non-repeating result; stale parent + still-unassigned lane continues to fail. Workflow contract validation requires both timestamps/budgets to be wired. No cron or deployment authority changes.

### StockDividend backend migration executable — PR #31
- Platform `apps/stockdividend/README.md` documents that the backend image did not compile the migration source into an executable, blocking an operator-run migration path until the app image is corrected.
- StockDividend master `backend/Dockerfile` previously copied `cmd/migrate/main.go` into the runtime image as `migrate_src.go`; it did not build an executable. PR #31 builds a static `/app/migrate` executable alongside `/app/main` and copies both into the runtime image.

It also confirmed reconciliation #41591224283 (06:39:06Z) failed only in the StockDividend health lane.
- Recovery workflow_run [#41591467278](https://github.com/phanthienquoc/platform-infra/actions/runs/41591467278) successfully dispatched read-only inspection [#41591479745](https://github.com/phanthienquoc/platform-infra/actions/runs/41591479745). Snapshot PR #1124 merged at `eaa65a1cdb72fc9df19eb2617a8fc21a9518d714`; `debug/vps-latest.md` was generated at 06:41:37Z. This manual fallback is fresh runtime evidence but does not satisfy native event=schedule freshness.
- Fresh snapshot: node `tce-k3s-01` Ready/ARM64, K3s v1.36.3+k3s1, Docker 29.1.3, containerd 2.3.2-k3s2, disk 55%, ~19 GiB available RAM, runner services active/policy v14. Media backend/frontend, MicroFE Shell/WS and NATS are 1/1. Stock Admin two Pods and the new Backend Pod remain ImagePullBackOff on old tag `12d63b4`; old Backend and legacy Frontend remain 1/1. TCE, Auth and Keycloak workloads are 0/0; public OIDC probes return HTTP 503. Host updates remain pending with restart_required=yes; no maintenance performed.
- Code review found a correctness gap: the scheduled inspection watchdog enforced freshness but did not reject a recent completed run whose conclusion was failure/cancelled. The recovery controller likewise used `updated_at` freshness without checking conclusion, so a recent failed inspection could suppress its read-only fallback.
- Dedicated branch `fix/inspection-success-gates-2026-11-03` now makes the watchdog require completed+success (in-progress is reported as pending; push smoke warns on non-success while scheduled/manual checks fail closed). Recovery now treats any completed non-success inspection as immediately stale and dispatches the existing read-only `inspect-vps.yml` fallback; an in-progress inspection still prevents duplicate dispatch.
- Static workflow-contract validation now asserts both success gates and the fallback behavior. No schedule expression, production manifest, image reference, credential, database, desired replica, Pod, host or deployment authority changed. `reconcile-vps.yml` remains the sole production apply authority. TCE market-scan schedule unchanged.

## 2026-11-03 15:10 ICT / 08:10 UTC — MicroFE dispatch provenance hardening

- Platform PR #1126 merged at `deb42dc0503023624369f9069639e32b286230e4`; its post-merge manifest validation #41600572015 completed SUCCESS. TCE PR [#1018](https://github.com/phanthienquoc/TCE-dashboard/pull/1018) then merged at `bf1567a8e51cbd31fb4b539a9979b100dc831c86`.
- TCE PR #1018 adds `app`, source repository, source run ID, exact workflow path, source event, and full source head SHA to the `microfe-images-published` payload, and validates those fields before dispatch. Exact-head tests passed: MicroFE Auth, MicroFE WebSocket, backend unit tests and Prettier. Publisher/build/promotion jobs were skipped on the workflow-only PR; no image publication or production dispatch occurred. Post-merge TCE WF-02 #41601256516 and WF-05 #41601256601 passed; WF-01 #41601256781 was still running at the last query.
- Platform PR [#1127](https://github.com/phanthienquoc/platform-infra/pull/1127), branch `fix/microfe-publisher-provenance-2026-11-03`, adds a public Actions API provenance verifier for the TCE MicroFE producer. It checks source app/repository/head repository, run ID, exact workflow path, event (push or workflow_dispatch), master branch, full SHA, required successful test/release-handoff/ARM64 build-publish jobs, and dispatch-job state. The producer's run remains in progress while waiting for Platform promotion, so the receiver accepts it only after all build/test jobs succeed and the dispatch job is in progress; a completed source run is accepted only when dispatch verification also succeeded. This avoids a circular wait without accepting an unverified digest payload.
- PR #1127 adds mocked API regression tests for valid in-progress/completed runs, identity/event/branch/SHA mismatches, failed publisher jobs, dispatch-job state, and invalid payloads. It also extends static workflow validation and documents the per-repository token scope. At the last query, exact-head checks `lint` and `validate` were still in progress; do not merge until both pass and the PR head is rechecked.
- Credential scope correction: TCE's `PLATFORM_INFRA_TOKEN` needs `Contents: write` and `Actions: read` on Platform Infra because WF-08B polls promotion/reconciliation run metadata. StockDividend's current producer only checks repository access and sends a dispatch, so its separate token needs `Contents: write` only. Platform's MicroFE receiver reads the public TCE Actions API without a cross-repository token. Secret values were not read or changed.
- Historical TCE WF-08B run #41154490073 (2026-10-31) completed both tests and both ARM64 image builds successfully, but its promotion-wait job failed because Platform run #41192084543 required the then-configured `PLATFORM_INFRA_AUTOMATION_TOKEN`, which was empty. The current Platform promotion workflow uses its scoped `GITHUB_TOKEN`; the obsolete PAT is not required and must not be reintroduced. This historical failure is not evidence of a current production blocker.
- Current heads at this checkpoint: Platform master `deb42dc0503023624369f9069639e32b286230e4`; TCE master `bf1567a8e51cbd31fb4b539a9979b100dc831c86`; StockDividend `50ba0dfb67eb3dfaa376db0c752bbef5b0817bec`; Media `763fdcd1463fe4d95c81c93d1e7ae010f2cd0cd3`; Authentication `1f39b18d9dd286da9dde32a79c4da269b19d5cfd`. It does not publish new images, dispatch production promotion, modify production manifests, credentials, database, replicas, Pods, host state, or any schedule. `reconcile-vps.yml` remains the only production apply authority; TCE market-scan schedule unchanged.
